# Graph Report - gym-app  (2026-10-04)

## Corpus Check
- 2485 files · ~1,818,486 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 161 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .csv 107, (none) 25, .css 14)

## Summary
- 38329 nodes · 70947 edges · 4579 communities (1551 shown, 3028 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 91% EXTRACTED · 9% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6653 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f047bb9c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
